### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼性を重視する通信」、UDPは「速さと軽さを重視する通信」です。TCPは届いたか・順番どおりかを確認して再送するので確実ですが遅延が増え、UDPは確認を省くので速い代わりに届かないこともあります。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para limpiar tu rama local antes de compartirla: reordena, une o reescribe tus commits para que la historia quede lineal. Usa merge para integrar una rama que ya está publicada o que otros usan, porque conserva la historia tal como ocurrió y no reescribe commits que alguien más pueda tener.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输，保证数据有序、不丢不重地送到；UDP 不建立连接、不保证送达，但开销小、延迟低。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is still yours alone and you want a clean, linear history before sharing it, such as tidying up commits or pulling in the latest main.
Merge when the branch is already shared or others have based work on it, because rebasing rewrites commits and would force them to untangle the change.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```bash
bun test test/utils.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里包含这段字符的测试文件：

```bash
bun test utils
```

如果想只跑某个测试用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test -t "解析日期"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to durable storage before the change touches the actual data files. After a crash, the database replays the log to restore committed changes and discard incomplete ones, so data stays consistent without writing the full data pages on every commit.
```

        '<div class="k-row-value">¥' + t.price + '</div>';
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    stamp.textContent = trains.updated ? trains.updated : '';
  }
  function load(){
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('trains')).then(function(v){ render(v); });
  }
  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
})();

  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly writes", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1650 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="wps"><span class="k-none">Not yet</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Nightly hawdb write benchmark</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var wps = document.getElementById('wps');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  var list = document.getElementById('list');

  function render(nights) {
    list.innerHTML = '';
    if (!Array.isArray(nights) || nights.length === 0) {
      list.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">Each night\'s result lands here.</div>';
      wps.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">Not yet</span>';
      sub.textContent = 'Nightly hawdb write benchmark';
      return;
    }
    var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
    wps.textContent = last.writes;
    sub.textContent = last.night + ' · p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls;
    nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function (n) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      row.innerHTML =
        '<div class="k-row-main">' + n.night + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + n.p99 + ' · stalls ' + n.stalls + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value">' + n.writes + '</div>';
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }

  render(ducter.get('nights'));
  ducter.onChange(function () { render(ducter.get('nights')); });
  ducter.summary(ducter.get('nights') ? 'Night ' + ducter.get('nights').length + ' logged' : 'No nights yet');
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":"181k/s","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":0},{"night":"Night 3","writes":"182k/s","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}

function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];

}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: the page is on your shelf as "PRs waiting on someone", with the four PRs and a tick box on each. Ticks are saved, and the count drops as they land.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on someone", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1856 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div><div class="k-sum-sub">tick each one off as it lands</div></div>
<h1 class="k-h">My PRs waiting on someone</h1>
<div class="k-card"><ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul></div>
<style>
.k-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--d-line)}
.k-row input{width:20px;height:20px;flex:none}
.k-row.done .k-row-main{text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)}
.k-row-main{font-weight:600}
</style>
<script>
const items = ducter.get("items") || [];
let landed = {};
function render(){
  const list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  items.forEach(it => {
    const li = document.createElement("li");
    li.className = "k-row" + (landed[it.id] ? " done" : "");
    const cb = document.createElement("input");
    cb.type = "checkbox";
    cb.checked = !!landed[it.id];
    cb.onchange = () => {
      landed[it.id] = cb.checked;
      ducter.set("landed", landed);
      render();
    };
    const main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = it.title;
    const sub = document.createElement("div");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = it.sub;
    const wrap = document.createElement("div");
    wrap.appendChild(main);
    wrap.appendChild(sub);
    li.appendChild(cb);
    li.appendChild(wrap);
    list.appendChild(li);
  });
  const left = items.filter(it => !landed[it.id]).length;
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = left;
  ducter.summary(left + " waiting");
}
Promise.resolve(ducter.get("landed")).then(v => { landed = v || {}; render(); });
ducter.onChange(() => { Promise.resolve(ducter.get("landed")).then(v => { landed = v || {}; render(); }); });
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"wey-gu/ducter#412 · compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · retry on 502","sub":"CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"wey-gu/ducter#418 · apps bar","sub":"Draft, not yet waiting on anyone"}]}

<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">出差花销合计</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没有花销，展开下面记第一笔</div>
<div id="actions"></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
<div class="k-card">
<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="如 11/20"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>分类</label><div class="k-chips" id="cats"></div></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-input k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</div>
</details>
</main>
<script>
const CATS=["交通","住宿","餐饮","其他"];
let cat="交通", sel=-1;
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const esc=s=>String(s??"").replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;"}[c]));
const items=()=>ducter.get("items")||[];
function drawCats(){ $("cats").innerHTML=CATS.map(c=>`<button class="k-chip" type="button" data-c="${c … y").hidden = xs.length>0;
  $("list").innerHTML = xs.map((x,i)=>`<div class="k-row" data-i="${i}"><div class="k-row-main">${esc(x.trip)}<div class="k-row-sub">${esc(x.day)}</div></div><div class="k-row-sub">${esc(x.cat)}${x.note?"<br>"+esc(x.note):""}</div><div class="k-row-value">¥${Number(x.amt).toLocaleString("zh-CN")}</div></div>`).join("");
  $("actions").innerHTML = (sel>=0 && sel<xs.length) ? '<button class="k-btn" id="del">删除这一笔</button>' : "";
  ducter.summary(xs.length ? xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+total.toLocaleString("zh-CN") : "还没记");
}
$("list").onclick=e=>{const r=e.target.closest("[data-i]"); if(!r) return; const i=Number(r.dataset.i); sel = sel===i ? -1 : i; draw();};
$("actions").onclick=e=>{ if(e.target.id!=="del") return; const xs=items(); xs.splice(sel,1); ducter.set("items",xs); sel=-1; draw(); };
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const trip=$("trip").value.trim(), amt=$("amt").value;
  if(!trip||!amt) return;
  const xs=items(); xs.push({trip, day:$("day").value.trim(), cat, amt:Number(amt), note:""});
  ducter.set("items",xs); $("amt").value=""; $("day").value="";
  document.querySelector("details").open=false; draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw); drawCats(); draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 上海虹桥→北京南 07:00"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20–21","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

完成标准：新测试通过；整套 TTL 测试连跑 20 次全部通过；把测试输出作为证据报告。

范围外：不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR，改动只留在现有分支 ducter/foo-ttl 上。不要改动 Cache.values()（另一个任务的内容）。
